    <script id="dotgg-sidebar-early-js">
        (function() {
            // Make config available to main script
            window.dotggSidebarInit = window.dotggSidebarInit || {};
            window.dotggSidebarInit.enabled = true;

            // Early collapsed state detection from cookie
            if (document.cookie.indexOf('dotgg-sidebar-collapsed=true') !== -1) {
                document.documentElement.classList.add('sidebar-collapsed-early');
            }

            // Fix for Blocksy theme sticky header bug
            // The sidebar's body padding can trigger Blocksy to think the page is scrolled
            if (typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined') {
                new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
                    for (var i = 0; i < mutations.length; i++) {
                        var m = mutations[i];
                        var header = m.type === 'attributes' ? m.target : document.querySelector('header[data-sticky]');
                        if (header) {
                            var val = header.getAttribute('data-sticky');
                            if (val && val.indexOf('fixed:') === 0) {
                                header.setAttribute('data-sticky', val.replace('fixed:', ''));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }).observe(document.documentElement, {
                    childList: true,
                    subtree: true,
                    attributes: true,
                    attributeFilter: ['data-sticky']
                });
            }

            // Sync sidebar logo row height with sticky header element height
            function syncHeaderHeight() {
                var header = document.querySelector('[data-sticky="yes:shrink"]') ||
                    document.querySelector('[data-sticky="shrink"]') ||
                    document.querySelector('[data-sticky*="shrink"]');
                if (header) {
                    var height = header.offsetHeight;
                    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--dotgg-header-height', height + 'px');
                }
            }

            // Run on DOM ready and after potential layout changes
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', syncHeaderHeight);
            } else {
                syncHeaderHeight();
            }
            window.addEventListener('load', syncHeaderHeight);
            window.addEventListener('resize', syncHeaderHeight);
        })();
    </script>

        /* ===========================================
           Table of Contents Plus — CLS fix
           ===========================================
           TOC+ ships the list EXPANDED in the server HTML, then collapses it in
           jQuery(document).ready() (visibility_hide_by_default=1): it runs
           ul.toc_list.hide() + #toc_container.addClass('contracted'). On throttled
           mobile that script runs seconds after first paint, so the whole article
           below the TOC jumps UP when the list collapses — the single largest
           layout shift on every guide/article page (~0.18 CLS, GSC "Need
           Improvement"). Proven with scripts/cls-rig (block front.min.js -> 0).

           Fix: pre-collapse the list at first paint so the late .hide() is a
           no-op (zero shift) while keeping the collapsed-by-default UX. The
           toggle still works — clicking [Show] runs jQuery .show() which sets an
           inline display:block that overrides this (non-!important) rule. */
        #toc_container ul.toc_list {
            display: none;
        }

        #toc_container.contracted {
            width: auto;
        }

                    <style>
            /* Shared Skeleton Animations */
            @keyframes skeletonPulse {
                0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
                50% { opacity: 0.7; }
            }

            /*
             * Sweep via transform (composited) -- NOT left/top/width.
             * Animating layout properties like left makes the Chrome Layout
             * Instability API count every shimmer frame as a real layout shift,
             * which wrecked deck-page CLS (~0.3 field). The ::after keeps its
             * base left:-100%; width:100%, so translateX(0)->(200%) reproduces
             * the exact -100%..+100% sweep with zero CLS. Do not revert to left.
             */
            @keyframes skeletonShimmer {
                0% { transform: translateX(0); }
                100% { transform: translateX(200%); }
            }

            /* Shared Skeleton Base Styles */
            .skeletonBlock {
                background: var(--theme-palette-color-6, #2a2a2a);
                border-radius: 8px;
                position: relative;
                overflow: hidden;
            }

            .skeletonBlock::after {
                content: "";
                position: absolute;
                top: 0;
                left: -100%;
                width: 100%;
                height: 100%;
                background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05), transparent);
                animation: skeletonShimmer 2s infinite;
            }

            /* Hide skeleton when content loads */
            [data-skeleton-type][style*="display: none"] {
                display: none !important;
            }
